- **reconnaissance-gate**: The actions above are a checklist. This is the home for the universal version (it extends, not replaces, the rich mvp `planning--reconnaissance-gate-SKILL.md` and `infrastructure-dis ([lubochka/xiigen-mvp-engine](https://github.com/lubochka/xiigen-mvp-engine/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/universal/reconnaissance-gate))
- **reconnaissance-internal-pentest**: How to approach local and internal network penetration testing. This is a reasoning framework that teaches methodology, not a tool reference. - **reconnaissance-javascript-analysis**: Use this playbook when analyzing any web application that serves JavaScript files. Modern SPAs (React, Vue, Angular, Next.js) expose almost all API routes and sometimes secrets inside JS bundles. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/reconnaissance-javascript-analysis))
- **reconnaissance-js-internal-hostname-intelligence**: Standard JS analysis extracts API endpoints and secrets. This skill goes deeper: extracting **internal hostnames** that reveal backend infrastructure topology, fleet naming conventions, internal service mesh references, and obfuscated hostnames (hex-encoded, base64-encoded, or split across variables). ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/reconnaissance-js-internal-hostname-intelligence))
- **reconnaissance-knowledge**: Comprehensive knowledge about network reconnaissance and service enumeration. Provides methodologies for port scanning, service fingerprinting, web directory discovery, and vulnerability identification. Includes best practices for structured data collection. ([aiskillstore/marketplace](https://github.com/aiskillstore/marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/charleskozel/reconnaissance-knowledge) · ★487 · 6 copies)
- **reconnaissance-monitoring-secrets-exposure**: Monitoring SDKs (Sentry, Datadog, Honeycomb, OpenTelemetry, New Relic, Rollbar, Bugsnag, LogRocket) are intentionally initialized client-side — their credentials land in every JS bundle. These credentials are write-keys by design, but they allow arbitrary event injection into production monitoring pipelines, enabling alert fatigue attacks, incident masking, and metric poisoning. ([kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s](https://github.com/kjkortes/a.e.g.i.s/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/offensive/airecon-fork/reconnaissance-monitoring-secrets-exposure))
